Proof of Lemma
14

Let us consider
\rho:=\sum_{j\in[\left|\mathcal{B}\right|^{n}]}\lambda_{j}|h_{j}\rangle\langle h_{j}|
, where
\{|h_{j}\rangle\}_{j\in\left|\mathcal{B}\right|^{n}}
is an orthonormal eigen basis of
\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{B}}^{\otimes n}
and
\{\lambda_{j}\}_{j\in\left|\mathcal{B}\right|^{n}}
is a distribution.
Applying the map
\mathcal{M}_{XY,a_{r},b_{t},\delta}
on
\rho
with any
(r,t)\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]^{2}
, we get the following operator:

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{XY,a_{r},b_{t},\delta}\left(\rho\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1+2\delta^{2}}\sum_{j\in[\left|\mathcal{B}\right|^{n}]}\lambda_{j}\left(|h_{j}\rangle\oplus\delta|h_{j}\rangle|a_{r}\rangle\oplus\delta|h_{j}\rangle|b_{t}\rangle\right)(\left\langle h_{j}|\oplus\delta\langle h_{j}|\langle a_{r}|\oplus\delta\langle h_{j}|\langle b_{t}|\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1+2\delta^{2}}\sum_{j\in[\left|\mathcal{B}\right|^{n}]}\lambda_{j}\left[\begin{array}[]{c c c }|h_{j}\rangle\langle h_{j}|&\delta|h_{j}\rangle\langle h_{j}|\langle a_{r}|&\delta|h_{j}\rangle\langle h_{j}|\langle b_{t}|\\
\delta|h_{j}\rangle|a_{r}\rangle\langle h_{j}|&\delta^{2}|h_{j}\rangle|a_{r}\rangle\langle h_{j}|\langle a_{r}|&\delta^{2}|h_{j}\rangle|a_{r}\rangle\langle h_{j}|\langle b_{t}|\\
\delta|h_{j}\rangle|b_{t}\rangle\langle h_{j}|&\delta^{2}|h_{j}\rangle|b_{t}\rangle\langle h_{j}|\langle a_{r}|&\delta^{2}|h_{j}\rangle|b_{t}\rangle\langle h_{j}|\langle b_{t}|\end{array}\right].

Now for each
j\in[\left|\mathcal{B}\right|^{n}]
, we have

\displaystyle\hskip 12.0pt{\left\lVert\mathcal{M}_{XY,a_{r},b_{t},\delta}\left(|h_{j}\rangle\langle h_{j}|\right)-\left[\begin{array}[]{cc}|h_{j}\rangle\langle h_{j}|&\left[\mathbf{0}\right]_{\left|\mathcal{B}\right|^{n}\times 2\left|\mathcal{A}\right|\left|\mathcal{B}\right|^{n}}\\
\left[\mathbf{0}\right]_{2\left|\mathcal{A}\right|\left|\mathcal{B}\right|^{n}\times\left|\mathcal{B}\right|^{n}}&\left[\mathbf{0}\right]_{2\left|\mathcal{A}\right|\left|\mathcal{B}\right|^{n}\times 2\left|\mathcal{A}\right|\left|\mathcal{B}\right|^{n}}\end{array}\right]_{\left|\mathcal{B}^{\prime}\right|\times\left|\mathcal{B}^{\prime}\right|}\right\rVert}_{1}

\displaystyle={\left\lVert\left[\begin{array}[]{c c c }\left[\mathbf{0}\right]_{\left|\mathcal{B}\right|\times\left|\mathcal{B}\right|}&\delta|h_{j}\rangle\langle h_{j}|\langle a_{r}|&\delta|h_{j}\rangle\langle h_{j}|\langle b_{t}|\\
\delta|h_{j}\rangle|a_{r}\rangle\langle h_{j}|&\delta^{2}|h_{j}\rangle|a_{r}\rangle\langle h_{j}|\langle a_{r}|&\delta^{2}|h_{j}\rangle|a_{r}\rangle\langle h_{j}|\langle b_{t}|\\
\delta|h_{j}\rangle|b_{t}\rangle\langle h_{j}|&\delta^{2}|h_{j}\rangle|b_{t}\rangle\langle h_{j}|\langle a_{r}|&\delta^{2}|h_{j}\rangle|b_{t}\rangle\langle h_{j}|\langle b_{t}|\end{array}\right]\right\rVert}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq 2\delta{\left\lVert|h_{j}\rangle\langle h_{j}|\langle a_{r}|\right\rVert}_{1}+2\delta{\left\lVert|h_{j}\rangle\langle h_{j}|\langle b_{t}|\right\rVert}_{1}+\delta^{2}({\left\lVert|h_{j}\rangle|a_{r}\rangle\langle h_{j}|\langle a_{r}|\right\rVert}_{1}+{\left\lVert|h_{j}\rangle|b_{t}\rangle\langle h_{j}|\langle b_{t}|\right\rVert}_{1}+2{\left\lVert|h_{j}\rangle|b_{t}\rangle\langle h_{j}|\langle a_{r}|\right\rVert}_{1})

\displaystyle=4\delta+4\delta^{2}\leq 8\delta.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle{\left\lVert\widehat{\rho}_{a_{r},b_{t},\delta}^{\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}-\tilde{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}\right\rVert}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{\left|\mathcal{B}\right|^{n}}\lambda_{j}{\left\lVert\mathcal{M}_{XY,a_{r},b_{t},\delta}\left(|h_{j}\rangle\langle h_{j}|\right)-\left[\begin{array}[]{cc}|h_{j}\rangle\langle h_{j}|&\left[\mathbf{0}\right]_{\left|\mathcal{B}\right|^{n}\times 2\left|\mathcal{A}\right|\left|\mathcal{B}\right|^{n}}\\
\left[\mathbf{0}\right]_{2\left|\mathcal{A}\right|\left|\mathcal{B}\right|^{n}\times\left|\mathcal{B}\right|^{n}}&\left[\mathbf{0}\right]_{2\left|\mathcal{A}\right|\left|\mathcal{B}\right|^{n}\times 2\left|\mathcal{A}\right|\left|\mathcal{B}\right|^{n}}\end{array}\right]_{\left|\mathcal{B}^{\prime}\right|\times\left|\mathcal{B}^{\prime}\right|}\right\rVert}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq 8\delta.

Appendix D
Proof of Lemma
7

For an
\left(\bar{x}^{n},\bar{y}^{n}\right):=((\bar{x}_{1},\bar{y}_{1}),(\bar{x}_{2},\bar{y}_{2}),\cdots,(\bar{x}_{n},\bar{y}_{n}))\in\mathcal{X}^{n}\times\mathcal{Y}^{n}
which has a form
\left(\bar{x}^{n},\bar{y}^{n}\right):=\Big(\underbrace{(1,1),\cdots,(1,1)}_{m_{1,1}},\underbrace{(1,2),\cdots,(1,2)}_{m_{1,2}},

\cdots,\underbrace{(\left|\mathcal{X}\right|,\left|\mathcal{Y}\right|),\cdots,(\left|\mathcal{X}\right|,\left|\mathcal{Y}\right|)}_{m_{\left|\mathcal{X}\right|,\left|\mathcal{Y}\right|}}\Big)
, where
\forall i\in[\left|\mathcal{X}\right|],j\in[\left|\mathcal{Y}\right|],m_{i,j}\geq 0
and
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\in[\left|\mathcal{X}\right|]\\
j\in[\left|\mathcal{Y}\right|]\end{subarray}}m_{i,j}=n
. Given
\left(\bar{x}^{n},\bar{y}^{n}\right)
, we consider the following state:

\widehat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{\bar{x}^{n},\bar{y}^{n}}:=\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{m_{1,1}}}_{U,m_{1,1}}\otimes\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{m_{1,2}}}_{U,m_{1,2}}\otimes\cdots\otimes\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{m_{\left|\mathcal{X}\right|,\left|\mathcal{Y}\right|}}}_{U,m_{\left|\mathcal{X}\right|,\left|\mathcal{Y}\right|}},

(261)

where
\forall i\in[\left|\mathcal{X}\right|],j\in[\left|\mathcal{Y}\right|],\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{m_{i,j}}}_{U,m_{i,j}}
is defined in (
21
). Now for any general pair
(x^{n},y^{n})\in\mathcal{X}^{n}\times\mathcal{Y}^{n}
, which is a permutation of
(\bar{x}^{n},\bar{y}^{n})
i.e.
x^{n}=\pi(\bar{x}^{n})=(\bar{x}_{\pi^{-1}(1)},\bar{x}_{\pi^{-1}(2)},\cdots,\bar{x}_{\pi^{-1}(n)})
and
y^{n}=\pi(\bar{y}^{n})=(\bar{y}_{\pi^{-1}(1)},\bar{y}_{\pi^{-1}(2)},\cdots,\bar{y}_{\pi^{-1}(n)})
, for some
\pi\in S_{n}
, we define
\hat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n},y^{n}}
as follows

\widehat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n},y^{n}}:=V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}(\pi)\left(\widehat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{\bar{x}^{n},\bar{y}^{n}}\right){V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}}^{\dagger}(\pi).

(262)

We define
\widehat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{y^{n}}
for any
y^{n}\in\mathcal{Y}^{n}
in an analogous way to how
\widehat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n}}
is defined for any
x^{n}\in\mathcal{X}^{n}
in (
23
). We now define the following states:

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}^{X^{n}Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{U,P_{X},P_{Y}}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x^{n}\in\mathcal{X}^{n}\\
y^{n}\in\mathcal{Y}^{n}\end{subarray}}P^{n}_{X}(x^{n})\overset{X^{n}}{|x^{n}\rangle\langle x^{n}|}\otimes P^{n}_{Y}(y^{n})\overset{Y^{n}}{|y^{n}\rangle\langle y^{n}|}\otimes\widehat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n},y^{n}},

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}^{X^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{U,P_{X}}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x^{n}\in\mathcal{X}^{n}\end{subarray}}P^{n}_{X}(x^{n})\overset{X^{n}}{|x^{n}\rangle\langle x^{n}|}\otimes\widehat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n}},

\displaystyle\widehat{\rho}^{Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{U,P_{Y}}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y^{n}\in\mathcal{Y}^{n}\end{subarray}}P^{n}_{Y}(y^{n})\overset{Y^{n}}{|y^{n}\rangle\langle y^{n}|}\otimes\widehat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{y^{n}}.

For
\widehat{R}_{1},\widehat{R}_{2}>0
, we define three projective measurement-based tests
\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X},\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,Y}
and
\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n}
as follows

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x^{n}\in\mathcal{X}^{n}\\
y^{n}\in\mathcal{Y}^{n}\end{subarray}}\overset{X^{n}}{|x^{n}\rangle\langle x^{n}|}\otimes\overset{Y^{n}}{|y^{n}\rangle\langle y^{n}|}\otimes\left\{\widehat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n},y^{n}}\succeq 2^{n\widehat{R}_{2}}\widehat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n}}\right\}\triangleq\left\{\widehat{\rho}^{X^{n}Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{U,P_{X},P_{Y}}\succeq 2^{n\widehat{R}_{2}}\left({\rho^{\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{\otimes n}}_{P_{Y}}}\otimes\rho^{X^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{U,P_{X}}\right)\right\},

(263)

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,Y}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x^{n}\in\mathcal{X}^{n}\\
y^{n}\in\mathcal{Y}^{n}\end{subarray}}\overset{X^{n}}{|x^{n}\rangle\langle x^{n}|}\otimes\overset{Y^{n}}{|y^{n}\rangle\langle y^{n}|}\otimes\left\{\widehat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n},y^{n}}\succeq 2^{n\widehat{R}_{1}}\widehat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{y^{n}}\right\}\triangleq\left\{\widehat{\rho}^{X^{n}Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{U,P_{X},P_{Y}}\succeq 2^{n\widehat{R}_{1}}\left({\rho^{\mathcal{H}_{X}^{\otimes n}}_{P_{X}}}\otimes\rho^{Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{U,P_{Y}}\right)\right\},

(264)

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x^{n}\in\mathcal{X}^{n}\\
y^{n}\in\mathcal{Y}^{n}\end{subarray}}\overset{X^{n}}{|x^{n}\rangle\langle x^{n}|}\otimes\overset{Y^{n}}{|y^{n}\rangle\langle y^{n}|}\otimes\left\{\widehat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n},y^{n}}\succeq 2^{n(\widehat{R}_{1}+\widehat{R}_{2})}\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{U,n}\right\}\triangleq\left\{\widehat{\rho}^{X^{n}Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{U,P_{X},P_{Y}}\succeq 2^{n(\widehat{R}_{1}+\widehat{R}_{2})}\left({\rho^{\mathcal{H}_{X}^{\otimes n}}_{P_{X}}}\otimes{\rho^{\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{\otimes n}}_{P_{Y}}}\otimes\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{U,n}\right)\right\}.

(265)

Since the states
\widehat{\rho}^{X^{n}Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{U,P_{X},P_{Y}},{\rho^{\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{\otimes n}}_{P_{Y}}}\otimes\rho^{X^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{U,P_{X}},{\rho^{\mathcal{H}_{X}^{\otimes n}}_{P_{X}}}\otimes\rho^{Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{U,P_{Y}}
,
and
\rho^{\mathcal{H}_{X}^{\otimes n}}_{P_{X}}\otimes\rho^{\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{\otimes n}}_{P_{Y}}\otimes\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{U,n}
are permutation invariant,
the matrices
\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X},\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,Y}
, and
\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n}
are permutation invariant. It is also important to note that like
\mathbb{T}_{n}
defined in (
24
), the expressions of
\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X},\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,Y}
and
\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n}
are independent of the distribution
P_{X},P_{Y}
. Now, for any
s^{n}\in\mathcal{S}^{n}
, we upper-bound the quantity
\mathrm{Tr}\left[\left(\mathbb{I}^{X^{n}Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}-\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X}\right)\rho^{X^{n}Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{P_{X},P_{Y},s^{n}}\right]
as follows

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\left[\left(\mathbb{I}^{X^{n}Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}-\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X}\right)\rho^{X^{n}Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{P_{X},P_{Y},s^{n}}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{a}{=}\mathrm{Tr}\left[\left(\mathbb{I}^{X^{n}Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}-\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X}\right)\frac{1}{\left|T_{Q_{s^{n}}}\right|}\sum_{\pi\in S_{n}}\rho^{X^{n}Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{P_{X},P_{Y},\pi(s^{n})}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Tr}\left[\left(\mathbb{I}^{X^{n}Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}-\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X}\right)\frac{1}{\left|T_{Q_{s^{n}}}\right|}\sum_{\widehat{s}^{n}\in T_{Q_{s^{n}}}}\rho^{X^{n}Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{P_{X},P_{Y},\widehat{s}^{n}}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{b}{\leq}(n+1)^{\left|\mathcal{S}\right|-1}\mathrm{Tr}\left[\left(\mathbb{I}^{X^{n}Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}-\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X}\right)\sum_{\widehat{s}^{n}\in T_{Q_{s^{n}}}}Q^{n}_{s^{n}}(\widehat{s}^{n})\rho^{X^{n}Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{P_{X},P_{Y},\widehat{s}^{n}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq(n+1)^{\left|\mathcal{S}\right|-1}\mathrm{Tr}\left[\left(\mathbb{I}^{X^{n}Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}-\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X}\right)\sum_{\widehat{s}^{n}\in\mathcal{S}^{n}}Q^{n}_{s^{n}}(\widehat{s}^{n})\rho^{X^{n}Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{P_{X},P_{Y},\widehat{s}^{n}}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{c}{=}(n+1)^{\left|\mathcal{S}\right|-1}\mathrm{Tr}\left[\left(\mathbb{I}^{X^{n}Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}-\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X}\right)\left(\rho^{{XY\mathcal{B}}}_{P_{X},P_{Y},Q_{s^{n}}}\right)^{\otimes n}\right]

\displaystyle=(n+1)^{\left|\mathcal{S}\right|-1}\mathrm{Tr}\left[\left(\mathbb{I}^{X^{n}Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}-\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X}\right)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x^{n}\in\mathcal{X}^{n}\\
y^{n}\in\mathcal{Y}^{n}\end{subarray}}P^{n}_{X}(x^{n})\overset{X^{n}}{|x^{n}\rangle\langle x^{n}|}\otimes P^{n}_{Y}(y^{n})\overset{Y^{n}}{|y^{n}\rangle\langle y^{n}|}\otimes\left(\bigotimes_{i=1}^{n}\rho^{\mathcal{B}}_{Q_{s^{n}},x_{i},y_{i}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\overset{d}{=}(n+1)^{\left|\mathcal{S}\right|-1}\sum_{x^{n}\in\mathcal{X}^{n}}P^{n}_{X}(x^{n})\mathrm{Tr}\left[\sum_{y^{n}\in\mathcal{Y}^{n}}P^{n}_{Y}(y^{n})\left(\bigotimes_{i=1}^{n}\rho^{\mathcal{B}}_{Q_{s^{n}},x_{i},y_{i}}\right)\left\{\widehat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n},y^{n}}\prec 2^{n\widehat{R}_{2}}\widehat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n}}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{e}{\leq}(n+1)^{\left|\mathcal{S}\right|-1}2^{nt\widehat{R}_{2}}\sum_{x^{n}\in\mathcal{X}^{n}}P^{n}_{X}(x^{n})\mathrm{Tr}\left[\sum_{y^{n}\in\mathcal{Y}^{n}}P^{n}_{Y}(y^{n})\left(\bigotimes_{i=1}^{n}\rho^{\mathcal{B}}_{Q_{s^{n}},x_{i},y_{i}}\right)(\widehat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n},y^{n}})^{-t}(\widehat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n}})^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{f}{\leq}(n+1)^{\left|\mathcal{S}\right|-1}2^{nt\widehat{R}_{2}}n^{\frac{t\left|\mathcal{X}\right|\left|\mathcal{Y}\right|(\left|\mathcal{B}\right|-1)\left|\mathcal{B}\right|}{2}}\left|\Lambda^{n}_{\left|\mathcal{B}\right|}\right|^{t\left|\mathcal{X}\right|\left|\mathcal{Y}\right|}\sum_{x^{n}\in\mathcal{X}^{n}}P^{n}_{X}(x^{n})\mathrm{Tr}\left[\sum_{y^{n}\in\mathcal{Y}^{n}}P^{n}_{Y}(y^{n})\left(\bigotimes_{i=1}^{n}\rho^{\mathcal{B}}_{Q_{s^{n}},x_{i},y_{i}}\right)^{1-t}(\widehat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n}})^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{g}{\leq}(n+1)^{\left|\mathcal{S}\right|-1+\frac{t\left|\mathcal{X}\right|\left|\mathcal{Y}\right|(\left|\mathcal{B}\right|-1)\left|\mathcal{B}\right|}{2}+(\left|\mathcal{B}\right|-1)t\left|\mathcal{X}\right|\left|\mathcal{Y}\right|}2^{nt\widehat{R}_{2}}\sum_{x^{n}\in\mathcal{X}^{n}}P^{n}_{X}(x^{n})\mathrm{Tr}\left[\bigotimes_{i=1}^{n}\left(\sum_{y\in\mathcal{Y}}P_{Y}(y)\left(\rho^{\mathcal{B}}_{Q_{s^{n}},x_{i},y}\right)^{1-t}\right)(\widehat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n}})^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{h}{\leq}(n+1)^{\left|\mathcal{S}\right|-1+\frac{t\left|\mathcal{X}\right|\left|\mathcal{Y}\right|(\left|\mathcal{B}\right|-1)\left|\mathcal{B}\right|}{2}+(\left|\mathcal{B}\right|-1)t\left|\mathcal{X}\right|\left|\mathcal{Y}\right|}2^{nt\widehat{R}_{2}}\sum_{x^{n}\in\mathcal{X}^{n}}P^{n}_{X}(x^{n})\left(\mathrm{Tr}\left[\left(\bigotimes_{i=1}^{n}\left(\sum_{y\in\mathcal{Y}}P_{Y}(y)\left(\rho^{\mathcal{B}}_{Q_{s^{n}},x_{i},y}\right)^{1-t}\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{1-t}}\right]\right)^{1-t}

\displaystyle=(n+1)^{\left|\mathcal{S}\right|-1+\frac{t\left|\mathcal{X}\right|\left|\mathcal{Y}\right|(\left|\mathcal{B}\right|-1)\left|\mathcal{B}\right|}{2}+(\left|\mathcal{B}\right|-1)t\left|\mathcal{X}\right|\left|\mathcal{Y}\right|}2^{nt\widehat{R}_{2}}\sum_{x^{n}\in\mathcal{X}^{n}}P^{n}_{X}(x^{n})\prod_{i=1}^{n}\left(\mathrm{Tr}\left[\left(\sum_{y\in\mathcal{Y}}P_{Y}(y)\left(\rho^{\mathcal{B}}_{Q_{s^{n}},x_{i},y}\right)^{1-t}\right)^{\frac{1}{1-t}}\right]\right)^{1-t}

\displaystyle=(n+1)^{\left|\mathcal{S}\right|-1+\frac{t\left|\mathcal{X}\right|\left|\mathcal{Y}\right|(\left|\mathcal{B}\right|-1)\left|\mathcal{B}\right|}{2}+(\left|\mathcal{B}\right|-1)t\left|\mathcal{X}\right|\left|\mathcal{Y}\right|}2^{nt\widehat{R}_{2}}\left(\sum_{x\in\mathcal{X}}P_{X}(x)\left(\mathrm{Tr}\left[\left(\sum_{y\in\mathcal{Y}}P_{Y}(y)\left(\rho^{\mathcal{B}}_{Q_{s^{n}},x,y}\right)^{1-t}\right)^{\frac{1}{1-t}}\right]\right)^{1-t}\right)^{n}

\displaystyle\overset{i}{=}(n+1)^{\left|\mathcal{S}\right|-1+\frac{t\left|\mathcal{X}\right|\left|\mathcal{Y}\right|(\left|\mathcal{B}\right|-1)\left|\mathcal{B}\right|}{2}+(\left|\mathcal{B}\right|-1)t\left|\mathcal{X}\right|\left|\mathcal{Y}\right|}2^{nt(\widehat{R}_{2}-I_{1-t}[Y;\mathcal{B}|X]_{P_{X},P_{Y},Q_{s^{n}}})}

\displaystyle{\leq}(n+1)^{\left|\mathcal{S}\right|-1+\frac{t\left|\mathcal{X}\right|\left|\mathcal{Y}\right|(\left|\mathcal{B}\right|-1)\left|\mathcal{B}\right|}{2}+(\left|\mathcal{B}\right|-1)t\left|\mathcal{X}\right|\left|\mathcal{Y}\right|}2^{nt(\widehat{R}_{2}-\min_{Q\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{S})}I_{1-t}[Y;\mathcal{B}|X]_{P_{X},P_{Y},Q})}

\displaystyle{\leq}f(n,\left|\mathcal{X}\right|,\left|\mathcal{Y}\right|,\left|\mathcal{B}\right|,\left|\mathcal{S}\right|,t)2^{nt(\widehat{R}_{2}-\min_{Q\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{S})}I_{1-t}[Y;\mathcal{B}|X]_{P_{X},P_{Y},Q})},

(266)

where
a
follows from the fact that
\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X}
is permutation invariant,
b
follows from (
28
),
c
follows from (
59
),
d
follows from the definition of
\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X}
in (
263
),

f
follows from arguments similar to prove Lemma
3
,

g
follows from Fact
2
,
h
follows from (
15
) of Fact
7
, and
i
follows from (
63
).
Here, the derivation of
e
is given as follows.
Since Lemma
2
guarantees that

\widehat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n},y^{n}}
commutes with
\widehat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n}}
,
we have
\left\{\widehat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n},y^{n}}-2^{n\widehat{R}_{2}}\widehat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n}}\prec 0\right\}\leq 2^{nt\widehat{R}_{2}}\left(\widehat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n},y^{n}}\right)^{-t}\left(\widehat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n}}\right)^{t}
for all
t\in(0,1)
,
which implies
e
.
Inequality (
266
) proves (
108
).

For any collection
\left\{\sigma_{x^{n}}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}\right\}_{\begin{subarray}{c}x^{n}\in\mathcal{X}^{n}\end{subarray}}\subset\mathcal{D}(\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{B}}^{\otimes n})
, we now upper-bound the quantity
\mathrm{Tr}\left[\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X}\left({\rho^{\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{\otimes n}}_{P_{Y}}}\otimes\sigma^{X^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{P_{X},\{\sigma^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n}}\}}\right)\right]
in the following discussions.
We define the subgroup
S_{n,x^{n}}:=\left\{\pi_{n}\in S_{n}:\pi(x^{n})=x^{n}\right\}
. Since
\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X}
is permutation invariant, any element
\pi\in S_{n,x^{n}}
satisfies
the relation

\displaystyle\hskip 13.0pt\mathrm{Tr}\left[\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X}\left(\rho^{\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{\otimes n}}_{P_{Y}}\otimes\left(\sum_{x^{n}\in\mathcal{X}^{n}}P^{n}_{X}(x^{n})\overset{X^{n}}{|x^{n}\rangle\langle x^{n}|}\otimes\sigma^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n}}\right)\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Tr}\left[\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X}\left(V^{X^{n}Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}(\pi)\left(\rho^{\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{\otimes n}}_{P_{Y}}\otimes\left(\sum_{x^{n}\in\mathcal{X}^{n}}P^{n}_{X}(x^{n})\overset{X^{n}}{|x^{n}\rangle\langle x^{n}|}\otimes\sigma^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n}}\right)\right){V^{X^{n}Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}}^{\dagger}(\pi)\right)\right]

\displaystyle\overset{a}{=}\mathrm{Tr}\left[\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X}\left(\rho^{\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{\otimes n}}_{P_{Y}}\otimes\left(\sum_{x^{n}\in\mathcal{X}^{n}}P^{n}_{X}(x^{n})\overset{X^{n}}{|\pi(x^{n})\rangle\langle\pi(x^{n})|}\otimes V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}(\pi)\left(\sigma^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n}}\right){V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}}^{\dagger}(\pi)\right)\right)\right]

\displaystyle\overset{b}{=}\mathrm{Tr}\left[\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X}\left(\rho^{\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{\otimes n}}_{P_{Y}}\otimes\left(\sum_{x^{n}\in\mathcal{X}^{n}}P^{n}_{X}(x^{n})\overset{X^{n}}{|x^{n}\rangle\langle x^{n}|}\otimes V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}(\pi)\left(\sigma^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n}}\right){V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}}^{\dagger}(\pi)\right)\right)\right],

(267)

where
a
follows from the fact that
\rho^{\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{\otimes n}}_{P_{Y}}
is permutation invariant and
b
follows from the property of
S_{n,x^{n}}
. Thus, we have the following

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\left[\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X}\left({\rho^{\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{\otimes n}}_{P_{Y}}}\otimes\sigma^{X^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{P_{X},\{\sigma^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n}}\}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\overset{a}{=}\frac{1}{\left|S_{n,x^{n}}\right|}\sum_{\pi\in S_{n,x^{n}}}\mathrm{Tr}\left[\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X}\left(\rho^{\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{\otimes n}}_{P_{Y}}\otimes\left(\sum_{x^{n}\in\mathcal{X}^{n}}P^{n}_{X}(x^{n})\overset{X^{n}}{|x^{n}\rangle\langle x^{n}|}\otimes V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}(\pi)\left(\sigma^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n}}\right){V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}}^{\dagger}(\pi)\right)\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Tr}\left[\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X}\left(\rho^{\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{\otimes n}}_{P_{Y}}\otimes\left(\sum_{x^{n}\in\mathcal{X}^{n}}P^{n}_{X}(x^{n})\overset{X^{n}}{|x^{n}\rangle\langle x^{n}|}\otimes\frac{1}{\left|S_{n,x^{n}}\right|}\sum_{\pi\in S_{n,x^{n}}}V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}(\pi)\left(\sigma^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n}}\right){V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}}^{\dagger}(\pi)\right)\right)\right]

\displaystyle\overset{b}{\leq}n^{\frac{\left|\mathcal{X}\right|\left|\mathcal{B}\right|(\left|\mathcal{B}\right|-1)}{2}}\left|\Lambda^{n}_{\left|\mathcal{B}\right|}\right|^{\left|\mathcal{X}\right|}\mathrm{Tr}\left[\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X}\left(\rho^{\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{\otimes n}}_{P_{Y}}\otimes\left(\sum_{x^{n}\in\mathcal{X}^{n}}P^{n}_{X}(x^{n})\overset{X^{n}}{|x^{n}\rangle\langle x^{n}|}\otimes\widehat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n}}\right)\right)\right]

\displaystyle\overset{c}{\leq}n^{\frac{\left|\mathcal{X}\right|\left|\mathcal{B}\right|(\left|\mathcal{B}\right|-1)}{2}}\left|\Lambda^{n}_{\left|\mathcal{B}\right|}\right|^{\left|\mathcal{X}\right|}2^{-n\widehat{R}_{2}}\mathrm{Tr}\left[\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X}\widehat{\rho}^{X^{n}Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{U,P_{X},P_{Y}}\right]\leq n^{\frac{\left|\mathcal{X}\right|\left|\mathcal{B}\right|(\left|\mathcal{B}\right|-1)}{2}}\left|\Lambda^{n}_{\left|\mathcal{B}\right|}\right|^{\left|\mathcal{X}\right|}2^{-n\widehat{R}_{2}}=g_{1}(n,\left|\mathcal{X}\right|,\left|\mathcal{B}\right|)2^{-n\widehat{R}_{2}},

(268)

where
a
follows from (
267
),
b
follows from Lemma
1
, and
c
follows from the definition of
\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X}
, given in (
263
).
Inequality (
268
) proves (
109
).

For
\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,Y}
, using approaches similar to the approaches discussed above to prove
eqs.

108
and
109
, one can prove
eqs.

110
and
111
. Further, for
\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n}
, the proofs of
eqs.

112
and
113
follow directly from the proofs of
eqs.

19
and
20
in Section
IV
.

Appendix E
Proof of Corollary
2

We choose the collection
\{\sigma^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n},y^{n}}\}_{\begin{subarray}{c}x^{n}\in\mathcal{X}^{n}\\
y^{n}\in\mathcal{Y}^{n}\end{subarray}}
in
\sigma^{X^{n}Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{P_{X},P_{Y},\{\sigma^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n},y^{n}}\}}
(defined in (
52
)) is to be
\{\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n},y^{n},s^{n}}\}
for an element
s^{n}\in\mathcal{S}^{n}
.
Using the discussion of the proof of Lemma
6
,
we have the following identities:

\displaystyle\sigma^{X^{n}Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{P_{X},P_{Y},\{\sigma^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n},y^{n}}\}}

\displaystyle\triangleq\rho^{X^{n}Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{P_{X},P_{Y},s^{n}},

\displaystyle\sigma^{X^{\prime}\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}_{P_{X},\{\sigma^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n}}\}}

\displaystyle\triangleq\rho^{X^{\prime}\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}_{P_{X},s^{n}},

\displaystyle\sigma^{Y^{\prime}\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}_{P_{Y},\{\sigma^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{y^{n}}\}}

\displaystyle\triangleq\rho^{Y^{\prime}\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}_{P_{Y},s^{n}},

\displaystyle\sigma^{\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\triangleq\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}_{s^{n}},

where
\sigma^{X^{\prime}\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}_{P_{X},\{\sigma^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n}}\}},\sigma^{Y^{\prime}\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}_{P_{Y},\{\sigma^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{y^{n}}\}}
and
\sigma^{\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}
are defined in
eqs.

237
,
238
and
239
. Now for any arbitrary
\delta\in(0,1)
,

eqs.

253
,
258
,
259
and
260
imply that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\left[\left(\mathbb{I}^{X^{\prime}Y^{\prime}\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}-{\mathbb{T}}^{\star}_{n}\right)\rho^{X^{\prime}Y^{\prime}\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}_{P_{X},P_{Y},s^{n}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 76\delta,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\left[{\mathbb{T}}^{\star}_{n}\left(\rho^{Y^{\prime}}_{P_{Y}}\otimes\rho^{X^{\prime}\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}_{P_{X},s^{n}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq g_{1}(n,\left|\mathcal{X}\right|,\left|\mathcal{B}\right|)2^{-n\widehat{R}_{2}+1},

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\left[{\mathbb{T}}^{\star}_{n}\left(\rho^{X^{\prime}}_{P_{X}}\otimes\rho^{Y^{\prime}\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}_{P_{Y},s^{n}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq g_{2}(n,\left|\mathcal{Y}\right|,\left|\mathcal{B}\right|)2^{-n\widehat{R}_{1}+1},

\displaystyle\mathrm{Tr}\left[{\mathbb{T}}^{\star}_{n}\left(\rho^{X^{\prime}}_{P_{X}}\otimes\rho^{Y^{\prime}}_{P_{Y}}\otimes\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}_{s^{n}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq g_{3}(n,\left|\mathcal{B}\right|)2^{-n(\widehat{R}_{1}+\widehat{R}_{2})+1}.

The above calculations hold for every
s^{n}\in\mathcal{S}^{n}
. This completes the proof of Corollary
2
.

Appendix F
Alternative Proof for Achievability of Theorem
3
(Random Code Capacity Region
\mathcal{R}_{r}
of a CQ-AVMAC)

(
This proof of achievability is established by designing a joint decoding strategy based on the simultaneous test designed in Lemma
6
in subsection

VI-D
.
)
We here show that for every rate-pair
(R_{1},R_{2})\in\mathcal{R}^{\star}
, there exists an
(n,2^{nR_{1}},2^{nR_{2}},\varepsilon_{n})
random code
\mathcal{C}_{n}
for which the average error probability,
averaged over the choice of code
\mathcal{C}_{n}
satisfies the following:

\max_{s^{n}\in\mathcal{S}^{n}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{C}_{n}}\left[\bar{e}(\mathcal{C}_{n},s^{n})\right]<\varepsilon_{n},

where
\lim_{n\to\infty}\varepsilon_{n}=0
. We now start with the construction of
\mathcal{C}_{n}
.

F-A

Randomized Encoder Construction

Alice and Bob randomly generate
2^{nR_{1}}
and
2^{nR_{2}}
sequences
\{X^{n}(m_{1})\in\mathcal{X}^{n}:m_{1}\in[2^{nR_{1}}]\}

and
\{Y^{n}(m_{2})\in\mathcal{Y}^{n}:m_{2}\in[2^{nR_{2}}]\}
according to the following distributions;

\displaystyle\Pr\left\{X^{n}(m_{1})\right\}

\displaystyle:=\prod_{i}P_{X}(X_{i}(m_{1}))\hbox{ with }X^{n}(m_{1}):=\left(X_{1}(m_{1}),\cdots,X_{n}(m_{1})\right),

(269)

\displaystyle\Pr\left\{Y^{n}(m_{2})\right\}

\displaystyle:=\prod_{i}P_{Y}(Y_{i}(m_{2}))\hbox{ with }Y^{n}(m_{2}):=\bigl(Y_{1}(m_{2}),\cdots,Y_{n}(m_{2})\bigr).

(270)

For the code
\mathcal{C}_{n}
, we define

\Pr\{\mathcal{C}_{n}\}:=\prod_{m_{1}=1}^{\mathcal{M}^{1}_{n}}\Pr\left\{X^{n}(m_{1})\right\}\cdot\prod_{m_{2}=1}^{\mathcal{M}^{2}_{n}}\Pr\left\{Y^{n}(m_{2})\right\}.

To send a message
m_{1}\in\mathcal{M}^{1}_{n}
, Alice encodes it to input sequence
X^{n}(m_{1})
and transmits it over the channel. Similarly, to end a message
m_{2}\in\mathcal{M}^{2}_{n}
, Bob encodes it to input sequence
Y^{n}(m_{2})
and transmits it over the channel.

F-B

Decoding Strategy

To construct our decoder, we
employ Lemma
6
, several relations appearing
in its proof, and Corollary
2
, which depend on the choice of

\widehat{R}_{1},\widehat{R}_{2}>0
and
t\in(0,1)
.
For this aim, we set the pair
(\widehat{R}_{1},\widehat{R}_{2})
as

\displaystyle R_{1}<\widehat{R}_{1}

\displaystyle<\min_{Q\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{S})}I[X;\mathcal{B}|Y]_{P_{X},P_{Y},Q},

(271)

\displaystyle R_{2}<\widehat{R}_{2}

\displaystyle<\min_{Q\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{S})}I[Y;\mathcal{B}|X]_{P_{X},P_{Y},Q},

(272)

\displaystyle R_{1}+R_{2}<\widehat{R}_{1}+\widehat{R}_{2}

\displaystyle<\min_{Q\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{S})}I[XY;\mathcal{B}]_{P_{X},P_{Y},Q}.

(273)

Then, we choose a sufficiently small real number
t\in(0,1)
such that

\displaystyle\widehat{R}_{1}

\displaystyle<\min_{Q\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{S})}I_{1-t}[X;\mathcal{B}|Y]_{P_{X},P_{Y},Q},

(274)

\displaystyle\widehat{R}_{2}

\displaystyle<\min_{Q\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{S})}I_{1-t}[Y;\mathcal{B}|X]_{P_{X},P_{Y},Q},

(275)

\displaystyle\widehat{R}_{1}+\widehat{R}_{2}

\displaystyle<\min_{Q\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{S})}I_{1-t}[XY;\mathcal{B}]_{P_{X},P_{Y},Q}.

(276)

Upon receiving the state
\rho_{\begin{subarray}{c}X^{n},Y^{n},s^{n}\end{subarray}}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}
, Charlie first embeds the state
\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{X^{n},Y^{n},s^{n}}
into
\mathcal{B}^{\prime}
using the way mentioned in (
224
).
We will represent this embedded state as
\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}_{X^{n},Y^{n},s^{n}}
. Charlie then generates two collections of i.i.d random variables
\tilde{R}:=\{R(i)\}_{i\in\mathcal{M}^{1}_{n}}
and
\tilde{T}:=\{T(j)\}_{j\in\mathcal{M}^{2}_{n}}
over the support set
[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]
, where

R(m_{1})
and
T(m_{2})
follow a uniform distribution over
[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]

for each
(m_{1},m_{2})\in\mathcal{M}^{1}_{n}\times\mathcal{M}^{2}_{n}
.
Using the notations
(X^{n},a_{R})(\hat{m}_{1}):=(X^{n}(\hat{m}_{1}),a_{R(\hat{m}_{1})})
and
(Y^{n},b_{T})(\hat{m}_{2}):=(Y^{n}(\hat{m}_{2}),b_{T(\hat{m}_{2})})

with
(\hat{m}_{1},\hat{m}_{2})\in\mathcal{M}^{1}_{n}\times\mathcal{M}^{2}_{n}
, we choose
the matrix
\mathbb{T}^{\star}_{n,\begin{subarray}{c}(X^{n},a_{R})(m_{1}),(Y^{n},b_{T})(m_{2}),\delta\end{subarray}}
as defined in (
235
).
Then, we define the POVM operator
\Lambda_{m_{1},m_{2}}^{\tilde{R},\tilde{T}}
as

\Lambda_{m_{1},m_{2}}^{\tilde{R},\tilde{T}}:=\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\hat{m}_{1}\in\mathcal{M}^{1}_{n}\\
\hat{m}_{2}\in\mathcal{M}^{2}_{n}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{T}^{\star}_{n,\begin{subarray}{c}(X^{n},a_{R})(\hat{m}_{1}),(Y^{n},b_{T})(\hat{m}_{2}),\delta\end{subarray}}\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbb{T}^{\star}_{n,\begin{subarray}{c}(X^{n},a_{R})(m_{1}),(Y^{n},b_{T})(m_{2}),\delta\end{subarray}}\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\hat{m}_{1}\in\mathcal{M}^{1}_{n}\\
\hat{m}_{2}\in\mathcal{M}^{2}_{n}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{T}^{\star}_{n,\begin{subarray}{c}(X^{n},a_{R})(\hat{m}_{1}),(Y^{n},b_{T})(\hat{m}_{2}),\delta\end{subarray}}\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}.

Charlie then uses the POVM
\left\{\Lambda_{m_{1},m_{2}}^{\tilde{R},\tilde{T}}\right\}
to decode the message.

F-C

Error Analysis

We now do the error analysis as follows. For every
s^{n}\in\mathcal{S}^{n}
, the following holds.

\displaystyle\hskip 10.0pt\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{R},\tilde{T}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{C}_{n}}\left[\bar{e}(\mathcal{C}_{n},s^{n})\right]=\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{1}+R_{2})}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m_{1}\in\mathcal{M}^{1}_{n}\\
m_{2}\in\mathcal{M}^{2}_{n}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{R},\tilde{T}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{C}_{n}}\left[e(m_{1},m_{2},\mathcal{C}_{n},s^{n})\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{1}+R_{2})}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m_{1}\in\mathcal{M}^{1}_{n}\\
m_{2}\in\mathcal{M}^{2}_{n}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{R},\tilde{T}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{C}_{n}}\left[e(m_{1},m_{2},\mathcal{C}_{n},s^{n})\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{1}+R_{2})}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m_{1}\in\mathcal{M}^{1}_{n}\\
m_{2}\in\mathcal{M}^{2}_{n}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{R},\tilde{T}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{C}_{n}}\left[\mathrm{Tr}\left[\left(\mathbb{I}^{\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}-\Lambda_{m_{1},m_{2}}^{\tilde{R},\tilde{T}}\right)\rho_{\begin{subarray}{c}X^{n}(m_{1}),Y^{n}(m_{2}),s^{n}\end{subarray}}^{\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\overset{a}{\leq}\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{1}+R_{2})}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m_{1}\in\mathcal{M}^{1}_{n}\\
m_{2}\in\mathcal{M}^{2}_{n}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{R},\tilde{T}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{C}_{n}}\left[\mathrm{Tr}\left[\left(\mathbb{I}^{\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}-\Lambda_{m_{1},m_{2}}^{\tilde{R},\tilde{T}}\right)\rho_{\begin{subarray}{c}(X^{n},a_{R})(m_{1}),(Y^{n},b_{T})(m_{2}),s^{n},\delta\end{subarray}}^{\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}\right]\right]+4\delta

\displaystyle\overset{b}{\leq}\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{1}+R_{2})}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m_{1}\in\mathcal{M}^{1}_{n}\\
m_{2}\in\mathcal{M}^{2}_{n}\end{subarray}}\Bigg(2\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{R},\tilde{T}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{C}_{n}}\left[\mathrm{Tr}\left[\left(\mathbb{I}^{\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}-\mathbb{T}^{\star}_{n,\begin{subarray}{c}(X^{n},a_{R})(m_{1}),(Y^{n},b_{T})(m_{2}),\delta\end{subarray}}\right)\rho_{\begin{subarray}{c}(X^{n},a_{R})(m_{1}),(Y^{n},b_{T})(m_{2}),s^{n},\delta\end{subarray}}^{\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 10.0pt+4\cdot\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\hat{m}_{1}\in\mathcal{M}^{1}_{n}:\\
\hat{m}_{1}\neq m_{1}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{R},\tilde{T}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{C}_{n}}\left[\mathrm{Tr}\left[\mathbb{T}^{\star}_{n,\begin{subarray}{c}(X^{n},a_{R})(\hat{m}_{1}),(Y^{n},b_{T})(m_{2}),\delta\end{subarray}}.\rho_{\begin{subarray}{c}(X^{n},a_{R})(m_{1}),(Y^{n},b_{T})(m_{2}),s^{n},\delta\end{subarray}}^{\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 10.0pt+4\cdot\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\hat{m}_{2}\in\mathcal{M}^{2}_{n}:\\
\hat{m}_{2}\neq m_{2}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{R},\tilde{T}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{C}_{n}}\left[\mathrm{Tr}\left[\mathbb{T}^{\star}_{n,\begin{subarray}{c}(X^{n},a_{R})(m_{1}),(Y^{n},b_{T})(\hat{m}_{2}),\delta\end{subarray}}\cdot\rho_{\begin{subarray}{c}(X^{n},a_{R})(m_{1}),(Y^{n},b_{T})(m_{2}),s^{n},\delta\end{subarray}}^{\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 10.0pt+4\cdot\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(\hat{m}_{1},\hat{m}_{2})\in\mathcal{M}^{1}_{n}\times\mathcal{M}^{2}_{n}:\\
\hat{m}_{1}\neq m_{1},\hat{m}_{2}\neq m_{2}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{R},\tilde{T}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{C}_{n}}\left[\mathrm{Tr}\left[\mathbb{T}^{\star}_{n,\begin{subarray}{c}(X^{n},a_{R})(\hat{m}_{1}),(Y^{n},b_{T})(\hat{m}_{2}),\delta\end{subarray}}\cdot\rho_{\begin{subarray}{c}(X^{n},a_{R})(m_{1}),(Y^{n},b_{T})(m_{2}),s^{n},\delta\end{subarray}}^{\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}\right]\right]\Bigg)+4\delta

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{1}+R_{2})}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m_{1}\in\mathcal{M}^{1}_{n}\\
m_{2}\in\mathcal{M}^{2}_{n}\end{subarray}}\Bigg(2\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}(X^{n},a_{R})(m_{1})\\
(Y^{n},b_{T})(m_{2})\end{subarray}}\left[\mathrm{Tr}\left[\left(\mathbb{I}^{\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}-\mathbb{T}^{\star}_{n,\begin{subarray}{c}(X^{n},a_{R})(m_{1}),(Y^{n},b_{T})(m_{2}),\delta\end{subarray}}\right)\rho_{\begin{subarray}{c}(X^{n},a_{R})(m_{1}),(Y^{n},b_{T})(m_{2}),s^{n},\delta\end{subarray}}^{\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 10.0pt+4\cdot\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\hat{m}_{1}\in\mathcal{M}^{1}_{n}:\\
\hat{m}_{1}\neq m_{1}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}(X^{n},a_{R})(\hat{m}_{1})\\
(Y^{n},b_{T})(m_{2})\end{subarray}}\left[\mathrm{Tr}\left[\mathbb{T}^{\star}_{n,\begin{subarray}{c}(X^{n},a_{R})(\hat{m}_{1}),(Y^{n},b_{T})(m_{2}),\delta\end{subarray}}\cdot\rho_{\begin{subarray}{c}(Y^{n},b_{T})(m_{2}),s^{n},\delta\end{subarray}}^{\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 10.0pt+4\cdot\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\hat{m}_{2}\in\mathcal{M}^{2}_{n}:\\
\hat{m}_{2}\neq m_{2}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}(X^{n},a_{R})(m_{1})\\
(Y^{n},b_{T})(\hat{m}_{2})\end{subarray}}\left[\mathrm{Tr}\left[\mathbb{T}^{\star}_{n,\begin{subarray}{c}(X^{n},a_{R})(m_{1}),(Y^{n},b_{T})(\hat{m}_{2}),\delta\end{subarray}}\cdot\rho_{\begin{subarray}{c}(X^{n},a_{R})(m_{1}),s^{n},\delta\end{subarray}}^{\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 10.0pt+4\cdot\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(\hat{m}_{1},\hat{m}_{2})\in\mathcal{M}^{1}_{n}\times\mathcal{M}^{2}_{n}:\\
\hat{m}_{1}\neq m_{1},\hat{m}_{2}\neq m_{2}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}(X^{n},a_{R})(\hat{m}_{1})\\
(Y^{n},b_{T})(\hat{m}_{2})\end{subarray}}\left[\mathrm{Tr}\left[\mathbb{T}^{\star}_{n,\begin{subarray}{c}(X^{n},a_{R})(\hat{m}_{1}),(Y^{n},b_{T})(\hat{m}_{2}),\delta\end{subarray}}\cdot\rho_{\begin{subarray}{c}s^{n},\delta\end{subarray}}^{\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}\right]\right]\Bigg)+4\delta

\displaystyle=4\delta+\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{1}+R_{2})}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m_{1}\in\mathcal{M}^{1}_{n}\\
m_{2}\in\mathcal{M}^{2}_{n}\end{subarray}}\Bigl(2\mathrm{Tr}\left[\left(\mathbb{I}^{X^{\prime}Y^{\prime}\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}-{\mathbb{T}}^{\star}_{n}\right)\rho^{X^{\prime}Y^{\prime}\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}_{P_{X},P_{Y},s^{n}}\right]+4\cdot 2^{nR_{1}}\mathrm{Tr}\left[{\mathbb{T}}^{\star}_{n}\left(\rho^{X^{\prime}}_{P_{X}}\otimes\rho^{Y^{\prime}\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}_{P_{Y},s^{n}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 95.0pt+4\cdot 2^{nR_{2}}\mathrm{Tr}\left[{\mathbb{T}}^{\star}_{n}\left(\rho^{Y^{\prime}}_{P_{Y}}\otimes\rho^{X^{\prime}\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}_{P_{X},s^{n}}\right)\right]+4\cdot 2^{n(R_{1}+R_{2})}\mathrm{Tr}\left[{\mathbb{T}}^{\star}_{n}\left(\rho^{X^{\prime}}_{P_{X}}\otimes\rho^{Y^{\prime}\mathcal{B}^{\prime}}_{P_{Y},s^{n}}\right)\right]\Bigr)

\displaystyle\overset{c}{\leq}156\delta+4\cdot 2^{-n\left\{\widehat{R}_{1}-R_{1}-\frac{1}{n}\right\}}g_{2}(n,\left|\mathcal{Y}\right|,\left|\mathcal{B}\right|)+4\cdot 2^{-n\left\{\widehat{R}_{2}-R_{2}-\frac{1}{n}\right\}}g_{1}(n,\left|\mathcal{X}\right|,\left|\mathcal{B}\right|)

\displaystyle\hskip 10.0pt+4\cdot 2^{-n\left\{(\widehat{R}_{1}+\widehat{R}_{2})-(R_{1}+R_{2})-\frac{1}{n}\right\}}g_{3}(n,\left|\mathcal{B}\right|)

\displaystyle\overset{d}{\leq}157\delta,

(277)

where

a
follows from
Fact
14
, and (
228
),
b
follows from Fact
12
, and
c
follows from Corollary
2
and
d
follows because we have chosen
n
large enough such that
4\cdot 2^{-n\left\{\widehat{R}_{1}-R_{1}-\frac{1}{n}\right\}}g_{2}(n,\left|\mathcal{Y}\right|,\left|\mathcal{B}\right|)+4\cdot 2^{-n\left\{\widehat{R}_{2}-R_{2}-\frac{1}{n}\right\}}g_{1}(n,\left|\mathcal{X}\right|,\left|\mathcal{B}\right|)+4\cdot 2^{-n\left\{(\widehat{R}_{1}+\widehat{R}_{2})-(R_{1}+R_{2})-\frac{1}{n}\right\}}g_{3}(n,\left|\mathcal{B}\right|)\leq\delta.

Since
\delta\in(0,1)
is arbitrary, therefore,

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\max_{s^{n}\in\mathcal{S}^{n}}\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{R},\tilde{T}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{C}_{n}}\left[\bar{e}(\mathcal{C}_{n},s^{n})\right]=0.

(278)

This completes the proof for achievability of Theorem
3
using simultaneous test mentioned in Lemma
6
.

Appendix G
Proof of (
134
)

The relation (
134
) can be shown as follows;

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{C}_{n}}\left[\bar{e}^{X}(\mathcal{C}_{n},s^{n})\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{1}+R_{2})}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m_{1}\in\mathcal{M}^{1}_{n}\\
m_{2}\in\mathcal{M}^{2}_{n}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{C}_{n}}\left[\mathrm{Tr}\left[\left(\mathbb{I}^{\mathcal{B}}-\left(\Lambda_{m_{1}}^{X}\right)\right)\rho_{\begin{subarray}{c}X^{n}(m_{1}),Y^{n}(m_{2}),s^{n}\end{subarray}}^{\mathcal{B}}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\overset{a}{\leq}\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{1}+R_{2})}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m_{1}\in\mathcal{M}^{1}_{n}\\
m_{2}\in\mathcal{M}^{2}_{n}\end{subarray}}\left(2\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{C}_{n}}\left[\mathrm{Tr}\left[\left(\mathbb{I}^{\mathcal{B}}-\Pi^{X}_{m_{1}}\right)\rho_{\begin{subarray}{c}X^{n}(m_{1}),Y^{n}(m_{2}),s^{n}\end{subarray}}^{\mathcal{B}}\right]\right]+4\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{C}_{n}}\left[\mathrm{Tr}\left[\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\hat{m}_{1}\in\mathcal{M}^{1}_{n}:\\
\hat{m}_{1}\neq m_{1}\end{subarray}}\Pi^{X}_{\hat{m}_{1}}\right)\rho_{\begin{subarray}{c}X^{n}(m_{1}),Y^{n}(m_{2}),s^{n}\end{subarray}}^{\mathcal{B}}\right]\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{1}+R_{2})}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m_{1}\in\mathcal{M}^{1}_{n}\\
m_{2}\in\mathcal{M}^{2}_{n}\end{subarray}}\left(2\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{C}_{n}}\left[\mathrm{Tr}\left[\left(\mathbb{I}^{\mathcal{B}}-\sum_{\hat{m}_{2}\in\mathcal{M}^{2}_{n}}\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X^{n}(m_{1}),Y^{n}(\hat{m}_{2})}^{X}\right)\rho_{\begin{subarray}{c}X^{n}(m_{1}),Y^{n}(m_{2}),s^{n}\end{subarray}}^{\mathcal{B}}\right]\right]\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 70.0pt\left.+4\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{C}_{n}}\left[\mathrm{Tr}\left[\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\hat{m}_{1}\in\mathcal{M}^{1}_{n}:\\
\hat{m}_{1}\neq m_{1}\end{subarray}}\sum_{\hat{m}_{2}\in\mathcal{M}^{2}_{n}}\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X^{n}(\hat{m}_{1}),Y^{n}(\hat{m}_{2})}^{X}\right)\rho_{\begin{subarray}{c}X^{n}(m_{1}),Y^{n}(m_{2}),s^{n}\end{subarray}}^{\mathcal{B}}\right]\right]\right)

\displaystyle\overset{b}{\leq}\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{1}+R_{2})}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m_{1}\in\mathcal{M}^{1}_{n}\\
m_{2}\in\mathcal{M}^{2}_{n}\end{subarray}}\left(2\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{C}_{n}}\left[\mathrm{Tr}\left[\left(\mathbb{I}^{\mathcal{B}}-\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X^{n}(m_{1}),Y^{n}(m_{2})}^{X}\right)\rho_{\begin{subarray}{c}X^{n}(m_{1}),Y^{n}(m_{2}),s^{n}\end{subarray}}^{\mathcal{B}}\right]\right]\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 70.0pt\left.+4\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\hat{m}_{1}\in\mathcal{M}^{1}_{n}:\\
\hat{m}_{1}\neq m_{1}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{C}_{n}}\left[\mathrm{Tr}\left[\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X^{n}(\hat{m}_{1}),Y^{n}(m_{2})}^{x}\rho_{\begin{subarray}{c}X^{n}(m_{1}),Y^{n}(m_{2}),s^{n}\end{subarray}}^{\mathcal{B}}\right]\right]\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 70.0pt\left.+4\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(\hat{m}_{1},\hat{m}_{2})\in\mathcal{M}^{1}_{n}\times\mathcal{M}^{2}_{n}\\
:\hat{m}_{1}\neq m_{1},\hat{m}_{2}\neq m_{2}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{C}_{n}}\left[\mathrm{Tr}\left[\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X^{n}(\hat{m}_{1}),Y^{n}(\hat{m}_{2})}^{X}\rho_{\begin{subarray}{c}X^{n}(m_{1}),Y^{n}(m_{2}),s^{n}\end{subarray}}^{\mathcal{B}}\right]\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{1}+R_{2})}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m_{1}\in\mathcal{M}^{1}_{n}\\
m_{2}\in\mathcal{M}^{2}_{n}\end{subarray}}\left(2\mathbb{E}_{X^{n}(m_{1}),Y^{n}(m_{2})}\left[\mathrm{Tr}\left[\left(\mathbb{I}^{\mathcal{B}}-\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X^{n}(m_{1}),Y^{n}(m_{2})}^{X}\right)\rho_{\begin{subarray}{c}X^{n}(m_{1}),Y^{n}(m_{2}),s^{n}\end{subarray}}^{\mathcal{B}}\right]\right]\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 70.0pt\left.+4\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\hat{m}_{1}\in\mathcal{M}^{1}_{n}:\\
\hat{m}_{1}\neq m_{1}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{E}_{X^{n}(\hat{m}_{1}),Y^{n}(m_{2})}\left[\mathrm{Tr}\left[\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X^{n}(\hat{m}_{1}),Y^{n}(m_{2})}^{X}\rho_{\begin{subarray}{c}Y^{n}(m_{2}),s^{n}\end{subarray}}^{\mathcal{B}}\right]\right]\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 70.0pt\left.+4\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(\hat{m}_{1},\hat{m}_{2})\in\mathcal{M}^{1}_{n}\times\mathcal{M}^{2}_{n}\\
:\hat{m}_{1}\neq m_{1},\hat{m}_{2}\neq m_{2}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{E}_{X^{n}(\hat{m}_{1}),Y^{n}(\hat{m}_{2})}\left[\mathrm{Tr}\left[\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n,X^{n}(\hat{m}_{1}),Y^{n}(\hat{m}_{2})}^{X}\rho_{\begin{subarray}{c}s^{n}\end{subarray}}^{\mathcal{B}}\right]\right]\right)

\displaystyle{\leq}2\mathrm{Tr}\left[\left(\mathbb{I}^{X^{n}Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}-\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n}^{X}\right)\rho^{X^{n}Y^{n}\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{P_{X},P_{Y},s^{n}}\right]+4\cdot 2^{nR_{1}}\mathrm{Tr}\left[\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n}^{X}\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x^{n}\in\mathcal{X}^{n}\\
y^{n}\in\mathcal{Y}^{n}\end{subarray}}P^{n}_{X}(x^{n})\overset{X^{n}}{|x^{n}\rangle\langle x^{n}|}\otimes P^{n}_{Y}(y^{n})\overset{Y^{n}}{|y^{n}\rangle\langle y^{n}|}\otimes\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{y^{n},s^{n}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 10.0pt+4\cdot 2^{n(R_{1}+R_{2})}\mathrm{Tr}\left[\hat{\mathbb{T}}_{n}^{X}\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x^{n}\in\mathcal{X}^{n}\\
y^{n}\in\mathcal{Y}^{n}\end{subarray}}P^{n}_{X}(x^{n})\overset{X^{n}}{|x^{n}\rangle\langle x^{n}|}\otimes P^{n}_{Y}(y^{n})\overset{Y^{n}}{|y^{n}\rangle\langle y^{n}|}\otimes\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{s^{n}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\overset{c}{\leq}2f(n,\left|\mathcal{X}\right|,\left|\mathcal{Y}\right|,\left|\mathcal{B}\right|,\left|\mathcal{S}_{\varepsilon}\right|,t)(1+\varepsilon)^{n}\left(2^{nt\left(\widehat{R}_{1}-\min_{Q\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{S}_{\varepsilon})}I_{1-t}[X;\mathcal{B}|Y]_{P_{X},P_{Y},Q}\right)}+2^{nt\left(\widehat{R}_{1}+\widehat{R}_{2}-\min_{Q\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{S}_{\varepsilon})}I_{1-t}[XY;\mathcal{B}]_{P_{X},P_{Y},Q}\right)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 10.0pt+4\cdot g_{2}(n,\left|\mathcal{Y}\right|,\left|\mathcal{B}\right|)2^{n(R_{1}-\widehat{R}_{1})}+4\cdot g_{3}(n,\left|\mathcal{B}\right|)2^{-n(\widehat{R}_{1}+\widehat{R}_{2}-R_{1}-R_{2})},

where
a
follows from Fact
12
,
b
follows from the fact that

and

Appendix H
Proof of Lemma
2

Consider the following series of equalities:

\displaystyle\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{U,n}\widehat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n}}=\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{U,n}V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}(\pi)\left(\widehat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{\bar{x}^{n}}\right){V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}}^{\dagger}(\pi)

\displaystyle\overset{a}{=}V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}(\pi)\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{U,n}{V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}}^{\dagger}(\pi)V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}(\pi)\left(\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{m_{1}}}_{U,m_{1}}\otimes\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{m_{2}}}_{U,m_{2}}\otimes\cdots\otimes\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{m_{\left|\mathcal{X}\right|}}}_{U,m_{\left|\mathcal{X}\right|}}\right){V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}}^{\dagger}(\pi)

\displaystyle\overset{b}{=}V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}(\pi)\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{U,n}\left(\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{m_{1}}}_{U,m_{1}}\otimes\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{m_{2}}}_{U,m_{2}}\otimes\cdots\otimes\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{m_{\left|\mathcal{X}\right|}}}_{U,m_{\left|\mathcal{X}\right|}}\right){V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}}^{\dagger}(\pi)

\displaystyle\overset{c}{=}V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}(\pi)\left(\sum_{\lambda\in\Lambda^{n}_{\left|\mathcal{B}\right|}}\frac{1}{\left|\Lambda^{n}_{\left|\mathcal{B}\right|}\right|}\frac{1}{\left|\mathcal{U}_{\lambda}\otimes\mathcal{V}_{\lambda}\right|}\Pi^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{\lambda}\right)\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(\lambda_{1},\cdots,\lambda_{\left|\mathcal{X}\right|})\\
\in(\times_{i\in[\left|\mathcal{X}\right|]}\Lambda^{m_{i}}_{\left|\mathcal{B}\right|})\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{\prod_{i\in[\left|\mathcal{X}\right|]}\left|\Lambda^{m_{i}}_{\left|\mathcal{B}\right|}\right|}\frac{1}{\prod_{i\in[\left|\mathcal{X}\right|]}\left|\mathcal{U}_{\lambda_{i}}\otimes\mathcal{V}_{\lambda_{i}}\right|}(\otimes_{i\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}\Pi^{\mathcal{B}^{m_{i}}}_{\lambda_{i}})\right){V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}}^{\dagger}(\pi)

\displaystyle=V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}(\pi)\left(\sum_{\lambda\in\Lambda^{n}_{\left|\mathcal{B}\right|}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(\lambda_{1},\cdots,\lambda_{\left|\mathcal{X}\right|})\\
\in(\times_{i\in[\left|\mathcal{X}\right|]}\Lambda^{m_{i}}_{\left|\mathcal{B}\right|})\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{\left|\Lambda^{n}_{\left|\mathcal{B}\right|}\right|}\frac{1}{\left|\mathcal{U}_{\lambda}\otimes\mathcal{V}_{\lambda}\right|}\frac{1}{\prod_{i\in[\left|\mathcal{X}\right|]}\left|\Lambda^{m_{i}}_{\left|\mathcal{B}\right|}\right|}\frac{1}{\prod_{i\in[\left|\mathcal{X}\right|]}\left|\mathcal{U}_{\lambda_{i}}\otimes\mathcal{V}_{\lambda_{i}}\right|}\Pi^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{\lambda}(\otimes_{i\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}\Pi^{\mathcal{B}^{m_{i}}}_{\lambda_{i}})\right){V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}}^{\dagger}(\pi)

\displaystyle\overset{d}{=}V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}(\pi)\left(\sum_{\lambda\in\Lambda^{n}_{\left|\mathcal{B}\right|}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(\lambda_{1},\cdots,\lambda_{\left|\mathcal{X}\right|})\\
\in(\times_{i\in[\left|\mathcal{X}\right|]}\Lambda^{m_{i}}_{\left|\mathcal{B}\right|})\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{\left|\Lambda^{n}_{\left|\mathcal{B}\right|}\right|}\frac{1}{\left|\mathcal{U}_{\lambda}\otimes\mathcal{V}_{\lambda}\right|}\frac{1}{\prod_{i\in[\left|\mathcal{X}\right|]}\left|\Lambda^{m_{i}}_{\left|\mathcal{B}\right|}\right|}\frac{1}{\prod_{i\in[\left|\mathcal{X}\right|]}\left|\mathcal{U}_{\lambda_{i}}\otimes\mathcal{V}_{\lambda_{i}}\right|}(\otimes_{i\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}\Pi^{\mathcal{B}^{m_{i}}}_{\lambda_{i}})\Pi^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{\lambda}\right){V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}}^{\dagger}(\pi)

\displaystyle=V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}(\pi)\left(\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{m_{1}}}_{U,m_{1}}\otimes\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{m_{2}}}_{U,m_{2}}\otimes\cdots\otimes\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{m_{\left|\mathcal{X}\right|}}}_{U,m_{\left|\mathcal{X}\right|}}\right)\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{U,n}{V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}}^{\dagger}(\pi)

\displaystyle=V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}(\pi)\left(\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{m_{1}}}_{U,m_{1}}\otimes\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{m_{2}}}_{U,m_{2}}\otimes\cdots\otimes\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{m_{\left|\mathcal{X}\right|}}}_{U,m_{\left|\mathcal{X}\right|}}\right){V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}}^{\dagger}(\pi)V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}(\pi)\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{U,n}{V^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}}^{\dagger}(\pi)=\widehat{\rho}^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{x^{n}}\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{U,n},

where
a
follows from the fact that
\rho^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{U,n}
is permutation invariant,
b
follows from. (
21
),
c
follows from (
8
) of Definition
9
.

Equality
d
follows from the following discussions.
We have two cases for the relation between
the elements
(\lambda_{1},\cdots,\lambda_{\left|\mathcal{X}\right|})\in(\times_{i\in[\left|\mathcal{X}\right|]}\Lambda^{m_{i}}_{\left|\mathcal{B}\right|})

and the young tableux
\lambda\in\Lambda^{n}_{\left|\mathcal{B}\right|}
.
In the first case,
the row-wise concatenation of
(\lambda_{1},\cdots,\lambda_{\left|\mathcal{X}\right|})

forms the young tableux
\lambda
.
In the second case,
the row-wise concatenation of
(\lambda_{1},\cdots,\lambda_{\left|\mathcal{X}\right|})

forms another young tableux
\lambda^{\prime}(\neq\lambda)
.
In the first case,
the support of
(\otimes_{i\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}\Pi^{\mathcal{B}^{m_{i}}}_{\lambda_{i}})
is a subspace of support of
\Pi^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{\lambda}
i.e.
(\otimes_{i\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}\Pi^{\mathcal{B}^{m_{i}}}_{\lambda_{i}})\preceq\Pi^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{\lambda}
.
Hence, we have
\Pi^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{\lambda}(\otimes_{i\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}\Pi^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{\lambda_{i}})=(\otimes_{i\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}\Pi^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{\lambda_{i}})\Pi^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{\lambda}=(\otimes_{i\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}\Pi^{\mathcal{B}^{m_{i}}}_{\lambda_{i}})
.
In the second case,
the support of
(\otimes_{i\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}\Pi^{\mathcal{B}^{m_{i}}}_{\lambda_{i}})
is orthogonal to the support of
\Pi^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{\lambda}
,
because the support of
\Pi^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{\lambda}
is orthogonal to
\Pi^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{\lambda^{\prime}}
.
Hence, we have
\Pi^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{\lambda}(\otimes_{i\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}\Pi^{\mathcal{B}^{n}}_{\lambda_{i}})=0
.
For both cases, the equality holds for the inside of the summations before and after
d
, which implies
d
.

Appendix I
Proof of (
244
)

Consider the following series of inequalities:

\displaystyle\hskip 10.0pt{\left\lVert\mathcal{N}_{X,a_{r},\delta}(|h\rangle\langle h|)\right\rVert}_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\left|\mathcal{A}\right|(1+2\delta^{2})}\left({\left\lVert\sum_{t\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}\delta(|h\rangle\oplus\delta|h\rangle|a_{r}\rangle)\langle h|\langle b_{t}|\right\rVert}_{\infty}+{\left\lVert\sum_{t\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}\delta|h\rangle|b_{t}\rangle(\langle h|\oplus\delta\langle h|\langle a_{r}|)\right\rVert}_{\infty}+{\left\lVert\sum_{t\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}\delta^{2}|h\rangle\langle h||b_{t}\rangle\langle b_{t}|\right\rVert}_{\infty}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{a}{\leq}\frac{1}{\left|\mathcal{A}\right|(1+2\delta^{2})}\left({\left\lVert\sum_{t\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}\delta(|h\rangle\oplus\delta|h\rangle|a_{r}\rangle)\langle h|\langle b_{t}|\right\rVert}_{1}+{\left\lVert\sum_{t\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}\delta|h\rangle|b_{t}\rangle(\langle h|\oplus\delta\langle h|\langle a_{r}|)\right\rVert}_{1}+{\left\lVert\sum_{t\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}\delta^{2}|h\rangle\langle h||b_{t}\rangle\langle b_{t}|\right\rVert}_{\infty}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\left|\mathcal{A}\right|(1+2\delta^{2})}\left(2{\left\lVert\sum_{t\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}\delta(|h\rangle\oplus\delta|h\rangle|a_{r}\rangle)\langle h|\langle b_{t}|\right\rVert}_{1}+{\left\lVert\sum_{t\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}\delta^{2}|h\rangle\langle h||b_{t}\rangle\langle b_{t}|\right\rVert}_{\infty}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\left|\mathcal{A}\right|(1+2\delta^{2})}\left(2\delta\mathrm{Tr}\left[\sqrt{(|h\rangle\oplus\delta|h\rangle|a_{r}\rangle)(\langle h|\oplus\delta\langle h|\langle a_{r}|)}\right]\sqrt{\sum_{t\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}{\left\lVert|b_{t}\rangle\right\rVert}_{2}^{2}}+\delta^{2}{\left\lVert\sum_{t\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}|b_{t}\rangle\langle b_{t}|\right\rVert}_{\infty}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\left|\mathcal{A}\right|(1+2\delta^{2})}\left(2\delta{\left\lVert|h\rangle\oplus\delta|h\rangle|a_{r}\rangle\right\rVert}_{2}^{2}\sqrt{\sum_{t\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}{\left\lVert|b_{t}\rangle\right\rVert}_{2}^{2}}+\delta^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\left|\mathcal{A}\right|(1+2\delta^{2})}\left(3\delta{\left\lVert|h\rangle\oplus\delta|h\rangle|a_{r}\rangle\right\rVert}_{2}^{2}\sqrt{\sum_{t\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}{\left\lVert{|b_{t}\rangle}\right\rVert}_{2}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{3\delta(1+\delta^{2})\sqrt{\left|\mathcal{A}\right|}}{(1+2\delta^{2}\left|\mathcal{A}\right|}\leq\frac{3\delta\sqrt{\left|\mathcal{A}\right|}}{\left|\mathcal{A}\right|}=\frac{3\delta}{\sqrt{\left|\mathcal{A}\right|}},

where
a
follows from the inequality

{\left\lVert\mathcal{O}\right\rVert}_{\infty}\leq{\left\lVert\mathcal{O}\right\rVert}_{1}
for
an operator
\mathcal{O}
.

Appendix J
Proof of (
246
)

Consider the following series of inequalities:

\displaystyle\hskip 10.0pt{\left\lVert\mathcal{N}_{\delta}(|h\rangle\langle h|)\right\rVert}_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\left|\mathcal{A}\right|^{2}(1+2\delta^{2})}\left({\left\lVert\sum_{r\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|],t\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}\delta|h\rangle(\langle h|\langle a_{r}|\oplus\langle h|\langle b_{t}|)\right\rVert}_{\infty}+{\left\lVert\sum_{r\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|],t\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}\delta(|h\rangle|a_{r}\rangle\oplus|h\rangle|b_{t}\rangle)\langle h|\right\rVert}_{\infty}\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 25.0pt\left.+{\left\lVert\sum_{r\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|],t\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}\delta^{2}|h\rangle\langle h|(|a_{r}\rangle\oplus|b_{t}\rangle)(\langle a_{r}|\oplus\langle b_{t}|)\right\rVert}_{\infty}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\left|\mathcal{A}\right|^{2}(1+2\delta^{2})}\left(2{\left\lVert\sum_{r\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|],t\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}\delta|h\rangle(\langle h|\langle a_{r}|\oplus\langle h|\langle b_{t}|)\right\rVert}_{\infty}+{\left\lVert\sum_{r\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|],t\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}\delta^{2}|h\rangle\langle h|(|a_{r}\rangle\oplus|b_{t}\rangle)(\langle a_{r}|\oplus\langle b_{t}|)\right\rVert}_{\infty}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{a}{\leq}\frac{1}{\left|\mathcal{A}\right|^{2}(1+2\delta^{2})}\left(2{\left\lVert\sum_{r\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|],t\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}\delta|h\rangle(\langle h|\langle a_{r}|\oplus\langle h|\langle b_{t}|)\right\rVert}_{1}+{\left\lVert\sum_{r\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|],t\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}\delta^{2}|h\rangle\langle h|(|a_{r}\rangle\oplus|b_{t}\rangle)(\langle a_{r}|\oplus\langle b_{t}|)\right\rVert}_{\infty}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\left|\mathcal{A}\right|^{2}(1+2\delta^{2})}\left(2\delta\mathrm{Tr}\left[\sqrt{|h\rangle\langle h|}\right]\sqrt{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|],t\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]\\
r^{\prime}\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|],t^{\prime}\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]\end{subarray}}(\langle h|\langle a_{r}|\oplus\langle h|\langle b_{t}|)(|h\rangle|a_{r^{\prime}}\rangle\oplus|h\rangle|b_{t^{\prime}}\rangle)}\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 25.0pt\left.+\delta^{2}{\left\lVert\sum_{r\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|],t\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}(|a_{r}\rangle\oplus|b_{t}\rangle)(\langle a_{r}|\oplus\langle b_{t}|)\right\rVert}_{\infty}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\left|\mathcal{A}\right|^{2}(1+2\delta^{2})}\left(2\delta\sqrt{2\left|\mathcal{A}\right|^{3}}+\delta^{2}\left({\left\lVert\left|\mathcal{A}\right|\sum_{r\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}|a_{r}\rangle\langle a_{r}|\right\rVert}_{\infty}+{\left\lVert\left|\mathcal{A}\right|\sum_{t\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}|b_{t}\rangle\langle b_{t}|\right\rVert}_{\infty}+{\left\lVert\sum_{r\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|],t\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}|a_{r}\rangle\langle b_{t}|\right\rVert}_{\infty}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 30.0pt\left.\left.+{\left\lVert\sum_{r\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|],t\in[\left|\mathcal{A}\right|]}|b_{t}\rangle\langle a_{r}|\right\rVert}_{\infty}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\left|\mathcal{A}\right|^{2}(1+2\delta^{2})}\left(2\sqrt{2}\delta\left|\mathcal{A}\right|\sqrt{\left|\mathcal{A}\right|}+4\delta^{2}\left|\mathcal{A}\right|\right)\leq\frac{7\delta}{\sqrt{\mathcal{A}}},

where
a
follows from the relation
{\left\lVert\mathcal{O}\right\rVert}_{\infty}\leq{\left\lVert\mathcal{O}\right\rVert}_{1}
for any operator
\mathcal{O}
.
